The original checkout's uncommitted merge-outcome changes were copied into the isolated worktree and preserved in place. ([design4pro/planned.travel](https://github.com/design4pro/planned.travel/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/autonomy-rollout.md))
- **autonomy-runbook**: Kontroler je deterministicki Python program (`scripts/autonomy/`, samo standardna biblioteka) koji svakih 60 minuta skupi signale, vodi red zadataka u SQLite-u i, kad je to dopusteno, pokrece postojeci runner `scripts/agents/cli.mjs` pa provjere iz `npm run release:check`. Nista ovdje ne poziva model niti pise na GitHub sam od sebe: to rade adapteri s vlastitim identitetom, koji su zadano iskljuce ([danielrisavi77-create/Lekta](https://github.com/danielrisavi77-create/Lekta/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/autonomy-runbook.md))
- **autonomy-setup**: V2 replaces the four-planner default. The Python standard-library runtime performs coordination; `loop.sh` is a plain shell launcher with **no polling model**. Legacy tooling is retained for explicit `PEAR_LEGACY=1` use only. Do not run V1 and V2 simultaneously. ([Joshua-Song007/hackprinceton](https://github.com/Joshua-Song007/hackprinceton/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/autonomy-setup.md))

The autonomy & robot-software-architecture specialist for the THINK stage. - **autoresearch**: Autoresearch loop agent — the single managing agent that runs the open-ended improvement loop for any phase. Makes one focused change per iteration and judges it itself (no worker/evaluator fan-out), keeping or reverting against the current best. Its memory is a per-loop wiki it reads before and writes after every move. Never stops on its own judgement; always stops at the caps set at setup, on a human stop, or after repeated tool errors. Inspired by Andrej Karpathy's autoresearch (MIT). ([stanislavjiricek/neuroflow](https://github.com/stanislavjiricek/neuroflow/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch**: Overnight skill optimizer. Runs N iterations of the Karpathy autoresearch loop: generate variant → eval against binary test cases → commit if better → discard if not. Use to improve ralph, tester, architect, or any skill. Makes ~12-15 experiments/hour. Run with --allow-all --max-autopilot-continues 100 for overnight sessions. Triggered by: 'autoresearch', 'improve skill overnight', 'optimize skill', 'run experiments'. ([elihuvillaraus/skills](https://github.com/elihuvillaraus/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.agent.md) · ★3)
- **AutoResearch**: Autonomous quantitative strategy researcher. Runs experiment loops: modify config → backtest → evaluate → keep/discard → repeat. Inspired by karpathy/autoresearch, adapted for trading strategy discovery. ([louisleech10/quantitative_trading_system](https://github.com/louisleech10/quantitative_trading_system/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/autoresearch.agent.md) · ★2)
